# C01-C07 final executable identity and process-isolation inventory

## Identity and count units

- Format version: `2` (breaking internal artifact change).
- Source commit: `bdb65eac0115e3d81a3dd55aba5365600e190e20`.
- Build: `go version go1.25.0 windows/amd64`, `windows/amd64`, CPU `24`, GOAMD64 `v1`.
- Input format-1 JSON SHA-256: `aa56f631810e812f34ce167247c11e7741890862eccf2a7ef1475f530c9c1f80`.
- Input format-1 Markdown SHA-256: `1c4a73a1feb6aae330998121bae565bf2b38ced8933e95bd364177659a4025cf`.

The inventory keeps three count units distinct: top-level Go test declarations, named `t.Run` scenarios, and process-start observations. Process observations are a separate ledger and are populated by Story 002; a source signal is not treated as an observation or classification.

## Verification boundary

`go list` completed with 77 package identities at the pinned source commit. The current-head per-package `go test -list '^Test' -count=0` sweep completed successfully for every resolved package and confirmed all 595 top-level declarations. The 217 named scenarios remain source-declared identities; their focused runtime confirmation belongs to Story 002.

Classification, exact isolation reasons, focused runtime witnesses, clean-room validation, and PR CI are intentionally deferred to the dependent stories. This artifact contains no package migration, shared-support change, test rewrite, or PR #2331 content.
